1b1('bum): @##//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa stong phrag brgya pa/
1b2('bum): bam po nga bdun pa/de nas tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bus/bcom 
1b3('bum): ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das don gang gi 
1b4('bum): slad du/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po zhes bgyi ba 
1b5('bum): 'di la bdag kyang spobs pa skye'o//bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' 
2a1('bum): @##//stsal pa/shA ra dwa ti'i bu don gang gi phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po zhes bya ba de 
2a2('bum): la spobs pa skyed cig/shA ra dwa ti'i bus gsol pa/bcom ldan 'das bdag tu lta ba rab tu spang ba'i slad du sems can 
2a3('bum): rnams la chos ston pa dang /sems can du lta ba rab tu spang ba dang /srog tu lta ba rab tu spang pa dang /gang zag tu lta ba rab 
2a4('bum): tu spang ba dang /gso bar lta ba rab tu spang ba dang/skyes bur lta ba rab tu spang ba dag/shed can du lta ba rab tu spong ba dang /
2a5('bum): shed bdag tu lta ba rab tu sbang ba dang /byed pa por lta ba rab tu spang ba dad /byed du 'jug pa por lta ba rab tu spong ba 
2a6('bum): dang /
2a7('bum): slong ba por lta ba rab tu spang pa dad /yongs su slong ba por lta ba rab du spang ba dang /tshor ba por lta ba rab tu spongs ba dang /
2b1('bum): tshor du 'jug pa por lta ba rab tu spang ba dang /shes pa por lta ba rab tu spong ba dang /mthong ba por lta ba rab tu spang pa dang /chad par lta ba rab tu spang ba dag/rtag par lta ba rab tu spang pa 
2b2('bum): dang /yod par lta ba rab tu spangs pa dang /med par lta ba rab tu spanga' ba dang /phung por lta ba rab tu spang dang /khams su lta ba rab tu spang ba dadang /skye mched du lta ba rab tu spang ba 
2b3('bum): dang /rten cing 'brel bar 'byung bar lta ba rab tu spang ba da nga /bden par lta ba rab tu spang  ba dang /pha rol tu phyin par lta ba rab tu spangab dang /stong pa nyid du lta ba rab tu spang 
2b4('bum): ba dang /dran pa nye bar gzhag par lta ba rab tu spang/yang dag par spong bar lta ba rab tu spangs ba dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang par lta ba rab tu spang ma dang /dbang por lta ba rab tu spang
2b5('bum): ba dang /stobs su lta ba rab tu spang pa dag/byang chub kyi yan lag tu lta ba rab tu spang ba dang /'phags pa'i lam du lta ba rab tu spang bdang /'phags pa'i bden par
2b6('bum): lta ba rab tu spang ba dag/bsam gtan du lta ba rab tu spang pa dang /tshad med par lta ba rab tu spang ba na/gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug par lta ba rab tu spang pa 
2b7('bum): rnam par thar par lta ba rab tu spang dang /mthar gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa lta ba rab tu spang ba da nga /rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa
3a1('bum): @#//dang|smon pa med par lta ba rab tu spang ba dang /mngon par shes par lta ba rab tu spang pa dang /ting nge 'dzin du lta bar btu spang ba dang /gzungs kyi sgor lta ba rab 
3a2('bum): tu spang ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs su lta ba rab tu spang ba dang/mi 'jigs par lta ba rab tu spangs ba dang /so so yang dag par rig par lta ba rab tu spang dang /byams pa 
3a3('bum): chen por lta ba rab tu spang ba dag/snying rje chen por lta ba rab tu spong ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres par lta ba rab tu spang bdang /sems can yongs su smin par bya bar lta ba rab tu 
3a4('bum): spang ba dang /sangs rgyas gyi zhing yongs su dag par bya bar lta ba rab du spang ba dang /byang chub tu lta ba rab tu spang ba dang/sangs rgyas su lta ba rab tu spang pa dag/chos su lta ba rab tu 
3a5('bum): spang pa dang /dge 'dun du lta ba rab tu spang ba dad/chos kyi 'khor la bskor bar lta ba rab tu spang ba da /yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar lta ba rab tu spang pa'i slad du sems can 
3a6('bum): rnams la chos ston pa bgyid kyang de mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis bgyid de/de'i slad du byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po zhes bgyi'o//de nas tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor gyis 
3a7('bum): tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu la 'di skad ces smras so//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dA ti'i bu ci'i phyir na/byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po gzugs su lta bar 'gyur ba yin/tshor 
3a8('bum): bar lta bar 'gyur ba yin/'du shes su lta bar 'gyur ba yi/'du byed du lta bar 'gyur ba yin/rnam par shes par lta bar 'gyur ba yin//mig tu lta bar 'gyur ba yin/
3b1('bum): rna bar lta bar 'gyur ba yin/snar lta bar 'gyur ba yin/lcer lta bar 'gyur ba yin//lus su lta bar 'gyur ba yin/yid du lta bar 'gyur ba yin/gzugs su lta bar 'gyur ba yi//sgrar lta bar 
3b2('bum): 'gyur ba yin//drir lta bar 'gyur ba yin/ror lta bar 'gyur ba yin/reg tu lta bar 'gyur ba yin/chos su lta bar 'gyur ba yin /mig gi rnam par shes par lta bar 'gyur ba yin/rna ba'i 
3b3('bum): rnam par shes par lta bar gyur ba yin/sna'i rnam par shes par lta ba 'gyur ba yin/lce'i rnam par shes par lta par 'gyur ba yin/lus kyi rnam par shes par lta bar/'gyur ba yin/
3b4('bum): yid kyi rnam par shes par ltar 'gyur ba yin/mig gi 'dus te reg par lta bar 'gyur ba yi na/rna ba'i 'dus te reg par lta ba 'gyur ba yin /sna'i 'dus te reg par lta par 'gyur ba yi na/
3b5('bum): lce'i 'dus te reg par lta bur 'gyur ba yin/lus kyis 'dus te reg par lta ba'i 'gyur ba yin/yid kyi 'dus te reg par lta bas 'gyur ba yin/mig gi 'dos te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor bar lta bar 
3b6('bum): 'gyur ba yin /rna ba'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis 'tshor bar lta bar 'gyur ba yin/sna'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor bar lta bar 'gyur ba'o//lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyenagyis tshor bar 
3b7('bum): lta bar 'gyur ba yin/lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyi tshor bar lta bar 'gyur pa yin/yid kyi 'dus te regs pa'i rkyen gyis tshor bar blta bar 'gyur ba yin/sa'i khams su lta bar 'gyur ba yin /
3b8('bum): chu'i khams su lta bar 'gyur//ba yin /me'i khams su lta bar 'gyurab yin/rlung gi khams su lta bar 'gyur pa yin /nam mkha'i khams su lta bar 'gyur la/
4a1('bum): @#//lta bar 'gyur ba yin/ma rig par lta bar 'gyur ba yin/'du byed du lta bar 'gyur ba yin/rnam par shes par lta bar 'gyur ba yin /ming dang gzugs su lta bar 'gyur ba yin/skye 
4a2('bum): mched drug tu lta bar 'gyur ba yin/reg par lta bar 'gyur ba yino/tshor bar lta bar 'gyur ba yin/sreg par lta bar 'gyur ba yis/len par lta bar 'gyur ba yi/srid par lta bar 'gyur ba yi na/
4a3('bum): skye bar lta bar 'gyur ba yini/rga shir lta bar 'gyur ba yin/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin par lta bar 'gyur ba yini/tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin par lta par 'gyur ba yin/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin par
4a4('bum): bar 'gyur ba yin/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin paralta bar 'gyur ba yin/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin par lta yang 'gyur ba yin/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin par lha bar 'gyur ba yin/nang stong pa 
4a5('bum): nyid du lta bar 'gyur ba yi ni/phyi stong pa nyid du lta bar 'gyur ba yan/phyi nang stong pa nyid du lta ba 'gyur ba yin/stong pa nyid stong pa nyid du lta bar 'byung ba yin/chen po stong pa nyid du lta bar gyur ba yin/don 
4a6('bum): dam pa stong pa nyid du lta bar 'phyur ba yin/'dus byas stong pa nyid du lta bar 'gyar ba yin/'dus ma byas stong pa nyid du lta par 'gyur ba yin/mtha' las 'das pa stong pa nyid du lta b.ra'agyur ba yin/thog 
4a7('bum): ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid du lta bar/'gyur ba yin/dor ba med pa stong pa nyid du blta bar 'gyur ba yin/rang bzhin stong pa nyid du lta bar byur ba yin/chos thams cad stong pa nyid du lta bar /'gyur ba yin/rang gi 
4a8('bum): mtshan nyid stong pa nyid du lta ba 'byung ba yin/med mi gas pa stong pa nyid du lta par gyur ba yin/dngos po med pa stong pa nyid du bya bar 'gyur ba yin/ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid du lta par 'byung ba yin/dngos 
4b1('bum): po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid du lta bar 'gyur ba yin/dran pa nye bar gzhag par lta bar 'gyur ba yin/yang dag par spong bar lta bar 'gyur ba yin/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang par lta bar 'gyur ba yin /dbang por lta 
4b2('bum): bar 'gyur ba yin/stobs             lta bar 'gyur ba yin/byang chub kyi yan lag tu lta bar 'gyur ba yin/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad par lta bar 'gyur ba yi /'phags pa'i bden par lta bar  
4b3('bum): 'gyur ba yin/bsam gtan du lta bar 'gyur ba yin/tshad med par lta bar 'gyur ba yis/gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug par lta bar 'gyur ba min/rnam par thar par lta bar 'gyur ba yi/mthar 
4b4('bum): gyis gnas pa'i snyoms par 'jug paralta bar 'gyur ba yin/rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med par lta bar 'gyur pa yin/mngon par shes par lta bar 'gyur ba yino/
4b5('bum): ting nge 'dzin tu lta bar 'gyur ba yin;gzungs kyi sgor lta bar 'gyur ba yin/ de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs su lta bar 'gyur ba yin/mi 'jigs par lta bar 'gyur ba yan/so so yang dag par rig par lta 
4b6('bum): bar 'gyur bar yin/byams pa chen por lta bar 'gyur ba yin /snying rje chen por lta bar 'gyur pa yin /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres par lta bar 'gyur ba yin /sems can yongs su smin par bya bar lta bar 'gyur 
4b7('bum): ba yin/sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par bya bar lta bar 'gyurab yin/byang chub tu lta bar 'gyur ba yin/sangs rgyas su lta bar 'gyur ba yini/chos su lta bar 'gyur ba yi/dge 'dun du lta bar 
4b8('bum): 'gyur ba yin/chos kyi 'khor lo skor bar lta bar 'gyur ba yin/yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar lta bar 'gyur ba yi /smras pa/tshe dang ldan pa rab 'byor 'di la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' 
5a1('bum): @#//chen po shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod cing /thabs mi mkhas pas gzugs su dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//tshor bar dmigs shing dmigs pI 
5a2('bum): tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/'du shes su dmigs shing dmigs ba'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed don/'du byed du dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshil gyis lta ba skyed do/rnam par shes par dmigs shing dmigs 
5a3('bum): pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed de/mig tu dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshal gyis lta ba skyed do//rna bar dmigs shing dmigasapa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed to//snar dmigs shing dmigs pa'i rgyu la gyis lta ba 
5a4('bum): skyed do//lcer dmigs shing dmigs pa 'chul gyis lta ba skyed do/lus su dmigs shing dmigs  pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed//yid du dmigs shing dmigasapa'i 'tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/gzugs su 
5a5('bum): dmigs shing dmigs pa'i 'tshul gyis lta ba skyedado//sgrar dmigs shing dmigs pa' tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//drir dmigs shing dmigs pa'o tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//ror dmigs shing dmigs pa'i
5a6('bum): gyis lta bskyed do//reg tu dmigs shing dmigasapa'i chul gyis lta ba bskyed do//chos su dmigs shing dmigasapa'i rgyu la gyis lta ba skyed do/mig gi rnam par shes par dmigs/shing dmigs pa'i 
5a7('bum): tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/rna ba'i rnam par shes par dmigs shing dmigs ba'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/sna'i rnam par shes par dmig sa shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do //lce'i rnam par 
5a8('bum): shes par dmigs shing dmi gas pa'i tshul gyis lta bskyed do//lus kyi rnam par shes par dmigasashing dmigs pa'i tshul gyisalta ba skyed do//yid kyi rnam par shes par da migs shing dmigs pa'i tshul
5b1('bum): gyis lta ba skyed do////m+to+eg gi 'dus te reg par dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//rna ba'i 'dus te reg par dmigs/shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis/lta ba skyed do//sna'i 'dus te reg par dmig shing dmigs pa'i 
5b2('bum): tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//lha'i 'dus te reg pa rna dmigs shing dmigs pa'i chu la gyis lta ba skyed do//lus kyi 'dus te reg par dmig/sa shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyi lta ba skyed do//yid kyi 'dus te reg par da mig sa shing dmigs pa'i tshul
5b3('bum): gyis lta ba skyed do/m+te ga'i dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor bar dmigs shing dmigs pa 'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do //rna ba'i 'dus te regapa'i rkyen gyis tshor bar da dmigs shing dmigs pa'i
5b4('bum): phyis tshor bar dmigs shing migs pa'i tshul gyis lta bskyed do//lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis
5b5('bum): tshor bar dmigs shing dmi gas pa'i tshul gyis lta ba bskyed do//lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rgyan gyis tshor bar dmigs
5b6('bum): gyis lta ba skyed do//yang kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor bar dmigs shing gdamigs pa'i tshul gyis lta 
5b7('bum): bskyed do//lce'i 'dus de reg pa'i rnam pa gnyis pa rnam par dmigs shing dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul
6a1('bum): @#//gyis lta ba skyed do//rig par dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//tshor bar dmigs shing dmigs pa'i'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do //sred par dmigs shing dmigs ba'i 
6a2('bum): tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/len par dmigs shing dmigasapa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed de//srid par dmigs shing dmigasaba'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do;/skya bar dmigs shing dmigs: pa'i tshul gyis lta ba
6a3('bum): skyed do;;rga shir dmigs shing dmigs pa 'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do://sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin par dmigs shing dmig pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed//tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin par dmi gas shing 
6a4('bum): dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta (ba)skyed do//bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin paradamigs shing dmigs pa'i+i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa ra dmigs shing dmigs pa'a'i tshul gyis lta bskyed 
6a5('bum): do//bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin par dmikas shing dmigs ba'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin par de migs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta bskyed do//nang stong pa nyid du dmigs
6a6('bum): shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyisalta bskyed do//phyi stong pa nyid du dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshu la gyis lta ba skyed do//phyi nang stong pa nyid du dmigis shing dmigs pa'i tshul kyis lta ba skyed do//stong pa nyid 
6a7('bum): stong pa nyid du dmigs shid dmigs pa'i tshul gyis ltab skyed do//chen po stong pa nyid du dmigs zhing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lha bskyed do//don dam pa stong pa nyid du dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul 
6a8('bum): gyis lta bskyed do//'dus byas stong pa nyid du dmigs shangi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lte bskyed do//'dus ma byas stong pa nyid du dmi gas shing dmigs pa'i tshulagyis lta bskyed do//mtha' las 
6b1('bum): 'das pa stong pa nyid du dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//thog ma dang tha ma med pa stong pa nyid du dmigs/shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis blta ba skyed do//dor ba med pa stong pa nyid du de dmigs 
6b2('bum): shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis/lta ba skyed do//rang bzhin stong pa nyid du dmigs shig dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta pa skyed do//chos thams cad stong pa nyid du dmigs shin dmigs pa'i tshul gyis ltab skyed do//
6b3('bum): rang gi mtshan nyid stong pa nyid du dmigs zhing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis la ba skyed do//mi dmigs pa stong pa nyid du dmig shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyi lta ba skyed do//dngos po med pa'i stong pa nyid du 
6b4('bum): dmigs shing dmigs pa'i chul gyis lta ba spyod do//ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid du dmig sa shing dmigs pa'i tshul kyis lta ba skyed do//dngos pa med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid du mig sa shing dmigs pa'i chu la 
6b5('bum): gyis lta ba skyed do//dran pa nye bar gzhag par dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis ltu ba skyed do//yang dag par spong bar dmigs shing dmigs pa tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang par 
6b6('bum): dmigs shing dmigs pa 'di tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/dbang por dmigs shing dmigs pas 'phrul gyis lta ba skyed do/stobs su dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshal gyis lta ba skyed do/byang chub kyi 
6b7('bum): yan lag tu dmigs shing dmigs pa'i 'chul gyis lta ba skyed do/'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad par dmigs shing dmig sa pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/'apags pa'i bden par dmigs 
6b8('bum): shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyi lta ba skyed do//bsam gtan du dmigs shing dmigs pa'i bskyed do//chad med par dmigs shing dmigs ba'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do gzugs 
7a1('bum): @#//med pa'i snyoms par 'jug par dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed de//rnam par thar par dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta pa skyed do//mthar gyis gnas pa'i 
7a2('bum): snyoms par 'jug pa dgur dmigs shing dmigs pa' tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//rnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang /mchan ma med pa dang /smon pa med par dmigs shing dmigs ba'i 
7a3('bum): tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/mngon par shes par dmigs shing dmigasapa'i tshul gyis lta bskyed do//tang nge 'dzin du dmigs shing dmigs pa'o tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/gzungs kyi sgor dmigs 
7a4('bum): shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyisalta ba skyed do//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs su dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshal gyis lta ba skyed do/mi 'jigs par dmigs shing dmigs
7a5('bum): lta ba skyed do//so so yang dag par dmigs shing dmigs tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//byams pa chen por dmigs shing dmigs pa tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/snying rje chen por dmigs 
7a6('bum): shing dmigs ba'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres par dmigs shing dmig sa pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do sems can yongs su smin par bya bar dmigs shing dmigs 
7a7('bum): pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed de//sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongs su dag par bya bar dmigs shing migs pa'i tshul gyis lta ba bskyed do//byang chub tu dmigs shing dmigs ba'i tshul gyisalta ba 
7a8('bum): skyed do//sangs rgyas su dmigs shing dmigasapa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed no//chos su dmigs shing dmigasaba'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed da dge 'dun du dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis 
7b1('bum): lta ba skyed do//chos kyi 'khor lo skor bar dmigs shing dmigs pa'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do//yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar dmigs shing dmigs ba'i tshul gyis lta ba skyed do //tshe dang ldan pa 
7b2('bum): rab 'byor de'i phyir byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen chen po gzugs su lta bar 'gyur ba yin/tshor bar lta ba dang /'du shes su lta ba dang /'du byed du lta ba dang /rnam par shes par lta bar 
7b3('bum): 'gyur ba yin/de'i phyir mig tu lta bar 'gyur ba yin/rna bar lta ba dang /snar lta ba dang /lcer lta ba dang /lus su lta ba dang /yid du lta bar 'gyur ba yin/de'i phyir gzugs su lta bar 'gyur pa yin/
7b4('bum): sgrar lta ba dang /drir lta ba dang /ror lta ba dang /reg tu lta ba dang /chos su lta bar 'gyur ba yino/de'i phyir mig gi rnam par shes par lta bar 'gyur ba yin//rna ba'i rnam par shes par lta ba dang /sna'i 
7b5('bum): rnam par shes par lta ba dag /lce'i rnam par shes par lta pa dang /lus kyi rnam par shes par lta bdang /yid kyi rnam par shes par lte bar 'gyur ba yin/de'iphyir mig gi 'dus te reg par lta bar 
7b6('bum): 'gyur ba yino/rna ba'i 'dus te reg par lta ba dang /sna'i 'dus te reg par lta pa dang /lce'i 'dus te reg par lta bdang /lus kyi 'dus te reg par lta ba/yid kyi 'dus te reg par ltar bar 'gyur 
7b7('bum): ba yin/de'i phyir mig gi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor bar lta bar 'gyur ba yin/rna ba'i 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyas tshor bar lta ba dang /sna'i 'du te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor bar lta pa dang /lce'i 
7b8('bum): 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tsho bar lta ba dang /lus kyi 'dus te rig pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba lta ba dang /yid kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyi tshor bar lta bar 'gyur pa yin/de'i phyir sa'i khams su lta bar 
8a1('bum): @#//'gyur ba yin/chu'i khams su lta ba dang /me'i khams su lta ba dang /rlung gi khams su lta ba dang /nam mkha'i khams su lta ba dang /rnam par shes pa'i khams su lta bar 'gyur 
8a2('bum): ba yin/de'i phyir ma rig par lta bar 'gyur ba yin/'du byed du lta ba dang /rnam par she pa lta ba dang /ming dang gzugs su lta ba dang /skye mched drug tu lta ba dang /reg par lta ba dang /tshor bar

26b2('bum): sems can thams cad kyang ngo bo nyid stong pa yid la sbyar bar bya'o//bdagis kyang dngos po med pa'i ngo bo nyid stong pa nyid yang su rdzogs par bya'o//sem can thams cad kyang dngosapa med pa'i ngo bo nyid stongapa nyid la 
26b3('bum): sbyar bar bya'o//bdag kyang dran pa nye bar gzhag pa bzhi la gnas par bya'o//sems can mthas cad kyang dran pa nye bar gzhan pa bzhi la sbyar bar bya'o//bdags kyang yang dag par spong ba bzhi la gnas par bya'o//
26b4('bum): sems can thams cad yang yang dag par spong pa bzhi la sbyar bar bya'o//bdag kyang rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhi la gnas par bya'o //sems can thams cad kyang/rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa bzhil sbyar bar bya'o//bdagag kyang 
26b5('bum): dbang po lnga la gnas par bya'o//sem can mthas cad kyang dbang po lnga la sbyar bar bya'o//bdag kyangastobs lnga la gnas par bya'o/sems can thams cad kyang stobs lnga la sbyar bar bya'o/bdag kyang byang chub kyi 
26b6('bum): yan lag bdun la gnas par bya'o//semams can thams cad kyang byang chub gyi yan lag bdun la sbyar bar bya'o//bdagang 'phags pa'i lam yan lag brgyad pa la gnas par bya'o//semag can thams 
26b7('bum): cad kyang 'phags pa'i lam yan pag brgyad pa la sbyar bar bya'o//bdagakyang 'phags pa'i bden pa rnams la gnas par bya'o//sel can thams cad kyang 'phags pa'i bden parnams la sbyar bar bya'o//
26b8('bum): bdag kyang bsam gtan rnams la gnas par bya'o//sems can thams cad kyi bsam gtan rnams la sbyar bar bya'o//bdags kyang tshad med pa rnams la gnas bar bya'o//sems can thams cad kyang
27a1('bum): @#//tshad med pa rnams la sbyar bar bya'o//bdag kyang gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa rnams la gnas par bya'o//sems can thams cad kyang gzugs med pa'i snyom par 'jug 
27a2('bum): pa rnams la sbyar bar bya'o//bdag kyang rnam par thar pa brgyad la gnas par bya'o//sem can thams cad kyang rnam par thar pa brgyud la sbyar bar bya'o//bdags kyang mthar gyis gnas pa'i 
27a3('bum): snyoms par 'jug pa dgu la gnas par bya'o//semams can thams cad kyang mthar gyis gnas pa' snyoms par 'jug pa dgu byar bar bya'o//bdag kyangarnam par thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid dang 
27a4('bum): mtshan ma med pa dang /smon pa med pa la gnas par bya'o//seman can thams cad kyang rnams thar pa'i sgo stong pa nyid/mtshan ma med pa dang /smyon pa med pa la sbyar bar bya'o//bdag
27a5('bum): kyang mngon par shes pa rnams la gnas par ba'o//sems can thams can kyang mngon par shes pa rnam la sbyar bar bya'o//bdag kyang ting nge 'dzin rnams la gnas par bya'o//sems can
27a6('bum): thams cad kyang ting nge 'dzin rnams 'a sbyar bar bya'o//bdag yang gzungs kyi sgo rnams la gnas par bya'o //sems can thams cad kyang gzungs kyi sgo rnams las sbyar bar bya'o//bdag byang 
27a7('bum): de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu la gnas par bya'o//sem can thams cad kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobasabacu la sbyar bar bya'o//brdag kyang mi 'jigs pa bzhi la gnas par bya'o//

27b1('bum): bya'o//bdag kyang snying rje chen po la gnas par bya'o//sems can thams cad kyang snying rje chen po la sbyar bar bya'o//bdag kyang sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad la gnas par bya'o//sem can

27b3('bum): bgos pa zhes bya'o//tsha dang ldan pa shA ra dwi ti'i bu gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la spyod pa'i tshe/sbyin pa gang yongs su gtong ba de dag nyan thos dang

27b6('bum): ba'i sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i go cha'o//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu gzhan yang byang chung sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po/rnam pa thams cad mkhyenapa nyid dang ldan pa'i yid 'du bya bas/sbyin pa

28a1('bum): @#//pa yongs su gtong zhing /chos de dag la bzod pa dang /mos pa dang /rten pa 'di ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa la 
28a2('bum): spyod cing /sbyin pa yongs su gtong ba'i bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i go cha'o//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu gzhan yang byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po/sbyin pa yongs su gtong

44a1('bum): @#//nye bar bsgrubs te gnas so//de rnam pa thams cad du chud zad med pa'i skye mched las yang dag par 'das te/'du shes med 'du shes med min skye mched la nye bar bsgrubs 
44a2('bum): te gnas so//'di ni byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i ting nge 'dzin te/byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen po//de rnam pa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid dang ldan pa'i sems/snying 
44a3('bum): rje chen po//sngon du 'gro bas/nyon mongs pa rab tu spang ba'i phyir/bsam gtan dang /tshad med pa dang /gzugs med pa'i snyoms par 'jug pa de dag gzhan la brjod cing 'chad pa dang /

44b7('bum): dang po'i yan lag dang /bsam gtan gnyis pa'i yan lag dang /bsamagatan gsum pa'i yan lag dang /babasam gtan bzhi pa'i yan lag dang /byams pa dang /snying rje dang /dga'

45a1('bum): @#//ba'i rnam pa dang /bdag med pa'i rnam pa dang /zhi ba'i rnam pa dang /stong pa'i rnam pa dang /mtshan ma med pa'i rnam pa dang /smon pa med pa'i rnam par rab tu rtog kyang /bsam

308a7('bum): lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor bamad pa gang yin pa dang /lce'i 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /lci'i 'dus ta reg pa'i rkyen gyis chor ba dben pa gang yin pa dang
308a8('bum): lce'i 'dus te reg pa'i rgyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang/phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang /dbus gang yin pa
308b1('bum): de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu/lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs 
308b2('bum): lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rten gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dang sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rgyan gyis tshor ba dben pa'i phyir/
308b3('bum): byang chub sems dpa' sngon kyi mthar mi dmigs so//lus kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rgyan gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/byang chub kyi sems dpa' sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs so//lus gyi 'dus 
308b4('bum): te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor pa med pa'i phyir/byang chub tu sems dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so/lus kyi 'dus te reg pa 'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nying kyi phyir/byang chub sems pa'i phyi ma'i mthar 
308b5('bum): mi dmigs so//lus kyis 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyes tshor pa dben pa'i phyir/byang chub sems/dpa'i phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs pa'o//lus kyi 'dus te regs pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyidamed pa'i phyir/
308b6('bum): byang chub sems/lus kyi 'dus te reg ma'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir/byang chub
308b7('bum): gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems/lus kyis 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med ba'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so//lus kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen
308b8('bum): lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshorab ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/byang chub sem dpa' dbus su mi dmig so//de ci'i phyir zhe na/tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dwa ti'i bu/lus kyi 'dus te reg ba 
309a1('bum): @#//rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa dang /lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid dang /lus kyi 'dus te rig pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa dang /lus kyi 'dus ste reg pa'i 
309a2('bum): rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa la/sngon gyis mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs te/lus kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa yang gzhan ma yin/
309a3('bum): lus kyis 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/lus kyi 'dus te reg ma'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dbenapa yang gzhan ma yin/lus kyis 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyes 
309a4('bum): pa tshor ba ngo bo nyid med ba yang gzhan ma yin/byang chub sems dpa' yang gzhan ma yin/sngon gyi mtha' yang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' yang gzhan ma yin /dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no/
309a5('bum): tsha dang ldan pa sha ra dwa ti'i bu/de ltar na lus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rgyen gyis tshor ba med pa gang yin pa dang /lus kyi 'du sa te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid gang yin pa dang /lus
309a6('bum): kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rgyan gyis tshor ba dben pa gang yin pa dang /yus kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyan gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha'
309a7('bum): gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang /dbus gang yin pa de thams cad kyang gnyis su med de gnyis su byar med do//tshe dang ldan pa shA radwa ti'i bu/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen
309a8('bum): gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong ba nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dba' sngon gyi mthar mi
309b1('bum): dmigs so//yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs so//yid kyi 'du sa ta rag pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med 
309b2('bum): pa'i phyir/byang chub sems pa'i sngon gyi mthar mi dmigs/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rgyan gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyir/byang chub sem dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs so//yod 
309b3('bum): kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen kyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyi phyir/byang chub sems dbang phyi ma'i mthar mi dmigs swo//yid kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba dbe na pa'i phyir/byang chub 
309b4('bum): dpa' phyi ma'i mthar mi da migs so//yid kyi 'duste reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshorba ngo bo nyid med pa'i phyir/byang chub
309b5('bum): rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa'i phyar/byang chub sems dpa'dbus su mi dmigs so//yid gyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen byis tshor bstod pa nyid kyi phyir/byang cub sems dpa' dbus su mi dmigs//
309b6('bum): yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'irkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa'i phyir/byang chub sems
309b7('bum): dpa' dbus su mi dmigs so/de ci'i phyir zhe na/tsha dang ldan pa shA radwa tI'i bu/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyenagyis tshor ba med pa dang /yid kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rgyen gyis tshor bstod pa nyid 
309b8('bum): dang /yid kyi 'dus te deg pa'i rgyan gyis tshor ba dben ba dang /yid kyi 'dus ste reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med ba la/sngon gyi mtha' mi dmigs/phyi ma'i mtha' mi dmigs/dbus mi dmigs
310a1('bum): @#//te/yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyen gyis tshor ba med pa yang gzhan ma yin/yid kyi 'dus te reg ba'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong pa nyid kyang gzhan ma yin/yid kyis 'dus te reg pa'i 
310a2('bum): rkyen gyis tshor ba dben pa yang gzhan ma yin/yang kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rkyed gyis tshor ba ngo bo nyid med pa yang gzhan ma yi na/byang chub sems dpa' yang gzhan yin/sngon gyi mtha' 
310a3('bum): yang gzhan ma yin/phyi ma'i mtha' yang gzhan ma yin/dbus kyang gzhan ma yin no//tshe dang ldan pa shA ra dI ti'i bu/de ltar na yid kyi 'dus te reg pa'i rgyen gyis tshor ba med gang 
310a4('bum): yin pa dang /yid kyi 'dus te reg pa 'i rkyen gyis tshor ba stong ba nyid gang yin pa dang /yid kyi 'das te reg pa'i rkyen gyis chor ba dben pa gang yin pa dang /yid gyi 'dus te rag pa'i 
310a5('bum): rkyen gyis tshor bango bo nyid med pa gang yin pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' gang yin pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' gang yin pa dang /phyi ma'i mtha' gang yin pa dang dbus
